# Near misses and US drafts

C that is not linked and not counted: a starting point. One file per function,
`nonmatching/<program>/func_XXXXXXXX.c` (`<program>` is `frontbin`, `boot_elf`, `i5bootn` or `levels`,
the name is the PAL address), with a comment at its top saying where it came from and what still
differs. Move it into `src/` once the full build prints `MATCH` with it in place, and delete its
row here. `tests/check_repository.py` checks that every file has a row.

A draft that uses inline asm (beyond `.extern` size hints), `volatile` (beyond named hardware memory and the literal EE hardware register and scratchpad addresses AGENTS.md allows), a `#define` alias or a hardcoded address is a study aid, not a route to a match: those are not allowed in matched C, so such a function stays here until plain C matches.

Files that start with `/* US draft` are rac3-uya-decomp's C for that function (commit 13cf5c5),
with names moved to PAL addresses where the address maps are sure of them
(`tools/port_from_us.py`, docs/history/porting-from-us.md). They were not carried for the reason in their row:

- **flags**: the US match used flags of its own for this one function (a "single-function
  override" or `@ps2as` range), which this repository does not allow (docs/build/build-fidelity.md);
- **differs**: the PAL function's bytes are not the US ones, or the C did not build to them here;
- **symbols**: a name in the C has no PAL address yet.

`functional.tsv` lists the US drafts OpenRAC's native port of NTSC-U reads in place of the
assembly (`tools/us_names.py --candidates`, docs/build/versions.md): C that matched NTSC-U, for a
function NTSC-U has and src/us/ has no C for.

FE-A1 (2026-11-22): plain C is still 10 differences (tools/check.py) with its file's line (now src/frontbin/3EE160.c, `-mvu0-use-vf0-vf2 -O2 -G8 -fopt-stack -mno-check-zero-division -mno-split-addresses @ps2as`), the same with a struct layout, float locals, every store order, the result in a local, and `func_003ED988` called with or without the argument: the register choice is fixed before scheduling; NM-FE2 (2026-11-22): 6 differences (tools/check.py) with its file's line (src/frontbin/3EE160.c, `-mvu0-use-vf0-vf2 -O2 -G8 -fopt-stack -mno-check-zero-division -mno-split-addresses @ps2as`), the same with `--no-context`. The draft's body as plain stores, no pins or barriers, `func_003ED988();` first, then in the order 0x18 = 0.5f, 0x2C = 0, 0x34 = 0.01f, 0x1C = 0.5f, 8 = `D_001D9738`, 0x30 = 0x80F00000, 0x38 = 0.01f, then `return object;`. Every register now matches retail; only the second scheduling pass differs: retail puts `move $v0, $s0` before the vtable store (ours puts the 0x1C store first) and `sw $zero, 0x2C` last (ours before the 0x34 store). All 5040 orders of the seven plain stores: 29 reach 6, none lower; the stores in pairs (split or chained, either order, 1920 forms), a struct layout, `func_003ED988(object)` and a float zero at 0x2C also stay at 6 |

| `func_L03_003C4A58` | levels | NTSC-U draft of `func_L03_003C2C08` | near miss: byte-exact on NTSC-U only with other flags when it was tried; with its file's current line it differs (NTSC-U twin `func_L03_003C2C08`; the draft's header has the worker's note); PAL (LV-F, 2026-11-21): the PAL C (Flavius's `nonmatching/functional/func_L03_003C4A58.c`, or `func_L03_003C6888`'s draft with this sibling's names: flag byte `D_00142795`, `D_001DED60_sda`, callees `func_L03_003C4558`/`003C4288`/`003C4850`/`003C4EE8`, sound `0x12AC`, `D_L03_001E9220[2] + 0.5f`, and no `func_00382468(10)` test) is 208 differences (tools/check.py, level 03, the file's flags), 209 at level 22; 178 differences (tools/check.py, level 03) with `-mvu0-use-vf0-vf2 @ps2as`. Blocker (shared by the whole ~1188-byte family): the masked `o[0x20]` compares. Retail keeps the masked state in `$a0` and the raw byte in `$a1` (`andi $a0,$v0,0xff; bnez $a0; move $a1,$v0`) and masks `$a1` again (`andi $v1,$a1,0xff`) at the join before the `== 2` test; this C has `$v1`/`$a0` and compares the copy unmasked, so one `andi` is missing and every later row shifts. Round 2026-11-21 (LV-F), measured on `func_L03_003C6888`'s draft (68 differences (tools/check.py, level 03) with `-mvu0-use-vf0-vf2 @ps2as`): a bitfield, a u8 struct field, `(u8)2` and `& 0xFF` stay at 68; a u8 or s32 local for the first, the second or both chains gives 279; of 15 flag variants, 7 stay at 68 (without `-fopt-stack`; `-fno-sched-interblock` with and without it; `-fno-rerun-loop-opt`, `-fno-strength-reduce`, `-fno-regmove`, `-fno-caller-saves`) and 8 are worse (`-mno-split-addresses` 218, `-fno-rerun-cse-after-loop` 280, `-fno-cse-follow-jumps` 281, `-fno-cse-skip-blocks` 169, `-fno-expensive-optimizations` 220, `-fno-schedule-insns` 230, `-fno-force-mem` 279, `-fno-delayed-branch` 322) Retry NM-R9 (Promises, 2026-11-22, upstream 6de86dfe): 208 differences (tools/check.py, level 03, the file's flags), 209 at level 22, 178 with `@ps2as` (`--as ps2as`), with `nonmatching/functional/func_L03_003C4A58.c`. Retail's opcode sequence is the same as `func_L03_003C77A0`'s except 6 instructions (no `func_00382468(10)` test). Why the join's `andi` is missing (RTL dumps, `-da`): gcse keeps `o[0x20]` in one QImode register copied from both chains' `lbu`; at the `== 2` test combine merges the copy, the `zero_extend` and the compare and drops the `zero_extend`, because both copies are from same-block byte loads (known high bits). Retail keeps it, so there either the extended value has a second use or one copy's source has unknown high bits. On 003C77A0 eight plain-C variants were all worse than 184: chain 1 as a `switch` 280, chain 2 as a `switch` 284, both 284, chain 1 as `if (!= 0) { if (== 1) } else` 243, a `u8 *` pointer to the state 245, a `u8 s = o[0x20]` local for chain 2 (two placements) 280, the same as `s32` 280 |
